\index{hsa\_ext\_amd.h@{hsa\_ext\_amd.h}!hsa\_amd\_memory\_pool\_t@{hsa\_amd\_memory\_pool\_t}}
\index{hsa\_amd\_memory\_pool\_t@{hsa\_amd\_memory\_pool\_t}!hsa\_ext\_amd.h@{hsa\_ext\_amd.h}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{hsa\_amd\_memory\_pool\_t}{hsa\_amd\_memory\_pool\_t}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily typedef struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structhsa__amd__memory__pool__s}{hsa\+\_\+amd\+\_\+memory\+\_\+pool\+\_\+s}} \mbox{\hyperlink{inc_2hsa__ext__amd_8h_ad7898ef210d13e71a2a490e97083c02d}{hsa\+\_\+amd\+\_\+memory\+\_\+pool\+\_\+t}}}



A memory pool encapsulates physical storage on an agent along with a memory access model. 

A memory pool encapsulates a physical partition of an agent\textquotesingle{}s memory system along with a memory access model. Division of a single memory system into separate pools allows querying each partition\textquotesingle{}s access path properties (see \mbox{\hyperlink{inc_2hsa__ext__amd_8h_a1a839a4273411c5a1812f72d170fbdc8}{hsa\+\_\+amd\+\_\+agent\+\_\+memory\+\_\+pool\+\_\+get\+\_\+info}}). Allocations from a pool are preferentially bound to that pool\textquotesingle{}s physical partition. Binding to the pool\textquotesingle{}s preferential physical partition may not be possible or persistent depending on the system\textquotesingle{}s memory policy and/or state which is beyond the scope of HSA APIs.

For example, a multi-\/node NUMA memory system may be represented by multiple pool\textquotesingle{}s with each pool providing size and access path information for the partition it represents. Allocations from a pool are preferentially bound to the pool\textquotesingle{}s partition (which in this example is a NUMA node) while following its memory access model. The actual placement may vary or migrate due to the system\textquotesingle{}s NUMA policy and state, which is beyond the scope of HSA APIs. \mbox{\Hypertarget{inc_2hsa__ext__amd_8h_a8b4736151eda8e9e0b3804a39294393c}\label{inc_2hsa__ext__amd_8h_a8b4736151eda8e9e0b3804a39294393c}}

{\footnotesize\ttfamily typedef bool($\ast$ hsa\+\_\+amd\+\_\+signal\+\_\+handler) (\mbox{\hyperlink{group__signals_ga67ca2818879c9990e1b5f1b14ce7ed27}{hsa\+\_\+signal\+\_\+value\+\_\+t}} value, void $\ast$arg)}



Asyncronous signal handler function type. 

Type definition of callback function to be used with hsa\+\_\+amd\+\_\+signal\+\_\+async\+\_\+handler. This callback is invoked if the associated signal and condition are met. The callback receives the value of the signal which satisfied the associated wait condition and a user provided value. If the callback returns true then the callback will be called again if the associated signal and condition are satisfied again. If the callback returns false then it will not be called again.


\begin{DoxyParams}[1]{Parameters}
\mbox{\texttt{ in}}  & {\em value} & Contains the value of the signal observed by hsa\+\_\+amd\+\_\+signal\+\_\+async\+\_\+handler which caused the signal handler to be invoked.\\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{ in}}  & {\em arg} & Contains the user provided value given when the signal handler was registered with hsa\+\_\+amd\+\_\+signal\+\_\+async\+\_\+handler\\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}

\begin{DoxyRetVals}{Return values}
{\em true} & resumes monitoring the signal with this handler (as if calling hsa\+\_\+amd\+\_\+signal\+\_\+async\+\_\+handler again with identical parameters)\\
\hline
{\em false} & stops monitoring the signal with this handler (handler will not be called again for this signal) \\
\hline
\end{DoxyRetVals}

Agents that do not support UUID will return the string \char`\"{}\+GPU-\/\+XX\char`\"{} or \char`\"{}\+CPU-\/\+XX\char`\"{} or \char`\"{}\+DSP-\/\+XX\char`\"{} depending upon their device type \mbox{\hyperlink{group__agentinfo_ga5e6c855643435ea1c2c7dc3fa2a123f0}{hsa\+\_\+device\+\_\+type\+\_\+t}} \\

\index{hsa\_ext\_amd.h@{hsa\_ext\_amd.h}!hsa\_amd\_agent\_memory\_pool\_info\_t@{hsa\_amd\_agent\_memory\_pool\_info\_t}}
\index{hsa\_amd\_agent\_memory\_pool\_info\_t@{hsa\_amd\_agent\_memory\_pool\_info\_t}!hsa\_ext\_amd.h@{hsa\_ext\_amd.h}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{hsa\_amd\_agent\_memory\_pool\_info\_t}{hsa\_amd\_agent\_memory\_pool\_info\_t}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily enum \mbox{\hyperlink{inc_2hsa__ext__amd_8h_ac75d52a1f581da87965bf2936d1e0256}{hsa\+\_\+amd\+\_\+agent\+\_\+memory\+\_\+pool\+\_\+info\+\_\+t}}}



Properties of the relationship between an agent a memory pool. 

\begin{DoxyEnumFields}{Enumerator}
\raisebox{\heightof{T}}[0pt][0pt]{\index{HSA\_AMD\_AGENT\_MEMORY\_POOL\_INFO\_ACCESS@{HSA\_AMD\_AGENT\_MEMORY\_POOL\_INFO\_ACCESS}!hsa\_ext\_amd.h@{hsa\_ext\_amd.h}}\index{hsa\_ext\_amd.h@{hsa\_ext\_amd.h}!HSA\_AMD\_AGENT\_MEMORY\_POOL\_INFO\_ACCESS@{HSA\_AMD\_AGENT\_MEMORY\_POOL\_INFO\_ACCESS}}}\mbox{\Hypertarget{inc_2hsa__ext__amd_8h_ac75d52a1f581da87965bf2936d1e0256a563310e03cf671ab29dd29ac074c95e6}\label{inc_2hsa__ext__amd_8h_ac75d52a1f581da87965bf2936d1e0256a563310e03cf671ab29dd29ac074c95e6}} 
HSA\+\_\+\+AMD\+\_\+\+AGENT\+\_\+\+MEMORY\+\_\+\+POOL\+\_\+\+INFO\+\_\+\+ACCESS&Access to buffers located in the memory pool. The type of this attribute is \mbox{\hyperlink{inc_2hsa__ext__amd_8h_aa4e03bb47a3f360e0cbedc22bae2b63b}{hsa\+\_\+amd\+\_\+memory\+\_\+pool\+\_\+access\+\_\+t}}.

An agent can always directly access buffers currently located in a memory pool that is associated (the memory\+\_\+pool is one of the values returned by \mbox{\hyperlink{inc_2hsa__ext__amd_8h_a744a0913e301d851d5272e7f3cc2c4f7}{hsa\+\_\+amd\+\_\+agent\+\_\+iterate\+\_\+memory\+\_\+pools}} on the agent) with that agent. If the buffer is currently located in a memory pool that is not associated with the agent, and the value returned by this function for the given combination of agent and memory pool is not HSA\+\_\+\+AMD\+\_\+\+MEMORY\+\_\+\+POOL\+\_\+\+ACCESS\+\_\+\+NEVER\+\_\+\+ALLOWED, the application still needs to invoke \mbox{\hyperlink{inc_2hsa__ext__amd_8h_ab332373131c6a381918a850d70c9fe04}{hsa\+\_\+amd\+\_\+agents\+\_\+allow\+\_\+access}} in order to gain direct access to the buffer.

If the given agent can directly access buffers the pool, the result is not HSA\+\_\+\+AMD\+\_\+\+MEMORY\+\_\+\+POOL\+\_\+\+ACCESS\+\_\+\+NEVER\+\_\+\+ALLOWED. If the memory pool is associated with the agent, or it is of fined-\/grained type, the result must not be HSA\+\_\+\+AMD\+\_\+\+MEMORY\+\_\+\+POOL\+\_\+\+ACCESS\+\_\+\+NEVER\+\_\+\+ALLOWED. If the memory pool is not associated with the agent, and does not reside in the global segment, the result must be HSA\+\_\+\+AMD\+\_\+\+MEMORY\+\_\+\+POOL\+\_\+\+ACCESS\+\_\+\+NEVER\+\_\+\+ALLOWED. \\
\hline

\raisebox{\heightof{T}}[0pt][0pt]{\index{HSA\_AMD\_AGENT\_MEMORY\_POOL\_INFO\_NUM\_LINK\_HOPS@{HSA\_AMD\_AGENT\_MEMORY\_POOL\_INFO\_NUM\_LINK\_HOPS}!hsa\_ext\_amd.h@{hsa\_ext\_amd.h}}\index{hsa\_ext\_amd.h@{hsa\_ext\_amd.h}!HSA\_AMD\_AGENT\_MEMORY\_POOL\_INFO\_NUM\_LINK\_HOPS@{HSA\_AMD\_AGENT\_MEMORY\_POOL\_INFO\_NUM\_LINK\_HOPS}}}\mbox{\Hypertarget{inc_2hsa__ext__amd_8h_ac75d52a1f581da87965bf2936d1e0256afed4990a3de75162ddd98fcbc8d4d05e}\label{inc_2hsa__ext__amd_8h_ac75d52a1f581da87965bf2936d1e0256afed4990a3de75162ddd98fcbc8d4d05e}} 
HSA\+\_\+\+AMD\+\_\+\+AGENT\+\_\+\+MEMORY\+\_\+\+POOL\+\_\+\+INFO\+\_\+\+NUM\+\_\+\+LINK\+\_\+\+HOPS&Number of links to hop when accessing the memory pool from the specified agent. The value of this attribute is zero if the memory pool is associated with the agent, or if the access type is HSA\+\_\+\+AMD\+\_\+\+MEMORY\+\_\+\+POOL\+\_\+\+ACCESS\+\_\+\+NEVER\+\_\+\+ALLOWED. \doxysubsection{Function Documentation}
\mbox{\Hypertarget{inc_2hsa__ext__amd_8h_a744a0913e301d851d5272e7f3cc2c4f7}\label{inc_2hsa__ext__amd_8h_a744a0913e301d851d5272e7f3cc2c4f7}} 
\index{hsa\_ext\_amd.h@{hsa\_ext\_amd.h}!hsa\_amd\_agent\_iterate\_memory\_pools@{hsa\_amd\_agent\_iterate\_memory\_pools}}
\index{hsa\_amd\_agent\_iterate\_memory\_pools@{hsa\_amd\_agent\_iterate\_memory\_pools}!hsa\_ext\_amd.h@{hsa\_ext\_amd.h}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{hsa\_amd\_agent\_iterate\_memory\_pools()}{hsa\_amd\_agent\_iterate\_memory\_pools()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \mbox{\hyperlink{group__status_gad755322e7ff95456520e8abdbe90d225}{hsa\+\_\+status\+\_\+t}} \mbox{\hyperlink{inc_2hsa__ext__image_8h_a64216765b98b62ee87deb50fd2899836}{HSA\+\_\+\+API}} hsa\+\_\+amd\+\_\+agent\+\_\+iterate\+\_\+memory\+\_\+pools (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{\mbox{\hyperlink{group__agentinfo_ga9ce217ef08e7d5e89c8e83ce7b6ef22b}{hsa\+\_\+agent\+\_\+t}}}]{agent,  }\item[{\mbox{\hyperlink{group__status_gad755322e7ff95456520e8abdbe90d225}{hsa\+\_\+status\+\_\+t}}($\ast$)(\mbox{\hyperlink{inc_2hsa__ext__amd_8h_ad7898ef210d13e71a2a490e97083c02d}{hsa\+\_\+amd\+\_\+memory\+\_\+pool\+\_\+t}} memory\+\_\+pool, void $\ast$data)}]{callback,  }\item[{void $\ast$}]{data }\end{DoxyParamCaption})}



Iterate over the memory pools associated with a given agent, and invoke an application-\/defined callback on every iteration. 

An agent can directly access buffers located in some memory pool, or be enabled to access them by the application (see \mbox{\hyperlink{inc_2hsa__ext__amd_8h_ab332373131c6a381918a850d70c9fe04}{hsa\+\_\+amd\+\_\+agents\+\_\+allow\+\_\+access}}), yet that memory pool may not be returned by this function for that given agent.

A memory pool of fine-\/grained type must be associated only with the host.


\begin{DoxyParams}[1]{Parameters}
\mbox{\texttt{ in}}  & {\em agent} & A valid agent.\\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{ in}}  & {\em callback} & Callback to be invoked on the same thread that called \mbox{\hyperlink{inc_2hsa__ext__amd_8h_a744a0913e301d851d5272e7f3cc2c4f7}{hsa\+\_\+amd\+\_\+agent\+\_\+iterate\+\_\+memory\+\_\+pools}}, serially, once per memory pool that is associated with the agent. The HSA runtime passes two arguments to the callback\+: the memory pool, and the application data. If {\ttfamily callback} returns a status other than \mbox{\hyperlink{group__status_ggad755322e7ff95456520e8abdbe90d225ae382ea0c9c05cce5a60d0317375159cc}{HSA\+\_\+\+STATUS\+\_\+\+SUCCESS}} for a particular iteration, the traversal stops and \mbox{\hyperlink{inc_2hsa__ext__amd_8h_a744a0913e301d851d5272e7f3cc2c4f7}{hsa\+\_\+amd\+\_\+agent\+\_\+iterate\+\_\+memory\+\_\+pools}} returns that status value.\\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{ in}}  & {\em data} & Application data that is passed to {\ttfamily callback} on every iteration. May be NULL.\\
\hline

\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{hsa\_amd\_agents\_allow\_access()}{hsa\_amd\_agents\_allow\_access()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \mbox{\hyperlink{group__status_gad755322e7ff95456520e8abdbe90d225}{hsa\+\_\+status\+\_\+t}} \mbox{\hyperlink{inc_2hsa__ext__image_8h_a64216765b98b62ee87deb50fd2899836}{HSA\+\_\+\+API}} hsa\+\_\+amd\+\_\+agents\+\_\+allow\+\_\+access (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{uint32\+\_\+t}]{num\+\_\+agents,  }\item[{const \mbox{\hyperlink{group__agentinfo_ga9ce217ef08e7d5e89c8e83ce7b6ef22b}{hsa\+\_\+agent\+\_\+t}} $\ast$}]{agents,  }\item[{const uint32\+\_\+t $\ast$}]{flags,  }\item[{const void $\ast$}]{ptr }\end{DoxyParamCaption})}



Enable direct access to a buffer from a given set of agents. 

Upon return, only the listed agents and the agent associated with the buffer\textquotesingle{}s memory pool have direct access to the {\ttfamily ptr}.

Any agent that has access to the buffer before and after the call to \mbox{\hyperlink{inc_2hsa__ext__amd_8h_ab332373131c6a381918a850d70c9fe04}{hsa\+\_\+amd\+\_\+agents\+\_\+allow\+\_\+access}} will also have access while \mbox{\hyperlink{inc_2hsa__ext__amd_8h_ab332373131c6a381918a850d70c9fe04}{hsa\+\_\+amd\+\_\+agents\+\_\+allow\+\_\+access}} is in progress.

The caller is responsible for ensuring that each agent in the list must be able to access the memory pool containing {\ttfamily ptr} (using \mbox{\hyperlink{inc_2hsa__ext__amd_8h_a1a839a4273411c5a1812f72d170fbdc8}{hsa\+\_\+amd\+\_\+agent\+\_\+memory\+\_\+pool\+\_\+get\+\_\+info}} with \mbox{\hyperlink{inc_2hsa__ext__amd_8h_ac75d52a1f581da87965bf2936d1e0256a563310e03cf671ab29dd29ac074c95e6}{HSA\+\_\+\+AMD\+\_\+\+AGENT\+\_\+\+MEMORY\+\_\+\+POOL\+\_\+\+INFO\+\_\+\+ACCESS}} attribute), otherwise error code is returned.


\begin{DoxyParams}[1]{Parameters}
\mbox{\texttt{ in}}  & {\em num\+\_\+agents} & Size of {\ttfamily agents}.\\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{ in}}  & {\em agents} & List of agents. If {\ttfamily num\+\_\+agents} is 0, this argument is ignored.\\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{ in}}  & {\em flags} & A list of bit-\/field that is used to specify access information in a per-\/agent basis. This is currently reserved and must be NULL.\\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{ in}}  & {\em ptr} & A buffer previously allocated using \mbox{\hyperlink{inc_2hsa__ext__amd_8h_a62edf07732d5dcc62f508011a8032581}{hsa\+\_\+amd\+\_\+memory\+\_\+pool\+\_\+allocate}}.\\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}

\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{hsa\_amd\_interop\_map\_buffer()}{hsa\_amd\_interop\_map\_buffer()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \mbox{\hyperlink{group__status_gad755322e7ff95456520e8abdbe90d225}{hsa\+\_\+status\+\_\+t}} \mbox{\hyperlink{inc_2hsa__ext__image_8h_a64216765b98b62ee87deb50fd2899836}{HSA\+\_\+\+API}} hsa\+\_\+amd\+\_\+interop\+\_\+map\+\_\+buffer (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{uint32\+\_\+t}]{num\+\_\+agents,  }\item[{\mbox{\hyperlink{group__agentinfo_ga9ce217ef08e7d5e89c8e83ce7b6ef22b}{hsa\+\_\+agent\+\_\+t}} $\ast$}]{agents,  }\item[{int}]{interop\+\_\+handle,  }\item[{uint32\+\_\+t}]{flags,  }\item[{size\+\_\+t $\ast$}]{size,  }\item[{void $\ast$$\ast$}]{ptr,  }\item[{size\+\_\+t $\ast$}]{metadata\+\_\+size,  }\item[{const void $\ast$$\ast$}]{metadata }\end{DoxyParamCaption})}



Maps an interop object into the HSA flat address space and establishes memory residency. The metadata pointer is valid during the lifetime of the map (until hsa\+\_\+amd\+\_\+interop\+\_\+unmap\+\_\+buffer is called). Multiple calls to hsa\+\_\+amd\+\_\+interop\+\_\+map\+\_\+buffer with the same interop\+\_\+handle result in multiple mappings with potentially different addresses and different metadata pointers. Concurrent operations on these addresses are not coherent. Memory must be fenced to system scope to ensure consistency, between mappings and with any views of this buffer in the originating software stack. 


\begin{DoxyParams}[1]{Parameters}
\mbox{\texttt{ in}}  & {\em num\+\_\+agents} & Number of agents which require access to the memory\\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{ in}}  & {\em agents} & List of accessing agents.\\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{ in}}  & {\em interop\+\_\+handle} & Handle of interop buffer (dmabuf handle in Linux)\\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{ in}}  & {\em flags} & Reserved, must be 0\\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{ out}}  & {\em size} & Size in bytes of the mapped object\\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{ out}}  & {\em ptr} & Base address of the mapped object\\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{ out}}  & {\em metadata\+\_\+size} & Size of metadata in bytes, may be NULL\\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{ out}}  & {\em metadata} & Pointer to metadata, may be NULL\\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}

\begin{DoxyRetVals}{Return values}
{\em HSA\+\_\+\+STATUS\+\_\+\+SUCCESS} & if successfully mapped\\
\hline
{\em HSA\+\_\+\+STATUS\+\_\+\+ERROR\+\_\+\+NOT\+\_\+\+INITIALIZED} & if HSA is not initialized\\
\hline
{\em HSA\+\_\+\+STATUS\+\_\+\+ERROR\+\_\+\+OUT\+\_\+\+OF\+\_\+\+RESOURCES} & if there is a failure in allocating necessary resources\\
\hline
{\em HSA\+\_\+\+STATUS\+\_\+\+ERROR\+\_\+\+INVALID\+\_\+\+ARGUMENT} & all other errors \\
\hline
\end{DoxyRetVals}

Imports shared memory into the local process and makes it accessible by the given agents. If a shared memory handle is attached multiple times in a process each attach may return a different address. Each returned address is refcounted and requires a matching number of calls to hsa\+\_\+amd\+\_\+ipc\+\_\+memory\+\_\+detach to release the shared memory mapping. 


\begin{DoxyParams}[1]{Parameters}
\mbox{\texttt{ in}}  & {\em handle} & Pointer to the identifier for the shared memory.\\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{ in}}  & {\em len} & Length of the shared memory to import. Reserved. Must be the full length of the shared allocation in this version.\\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{ in}}  & {\em num\+\_\+agents} & Count of agents in {\ttfamily mapping\+\_\+agents}. May be zero if all agents are to be allowed access.\\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{ in}}  & {\em mapping\+\_\+agents} & List of agents to access the shared memory. Ignored if {\ttfamily num\+\_\+agents} is zero.\\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{ out}}  & {\em mapped\+\_\+ptr} & Recieves a process local pointer to the shared memory.\\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}

\begin{DoxyRetVals}{Return values}
{\em HSA\+\_\+\+STATUS\+\_\+\+SUCCESS} & if memory is successfully imported.\\
\hline
{\em HSA\+\_\+\+STATUS\+\_\+\+ERROR\+\_\+\+NOT\+\_\+\+INITIALIZED} & if HSA is not initialized\\
\hline
{\em HSA\+\_\+\+STATUS\+\_\+\+ERROR\+\_\+\+OUT\+\_\+\+OF\+\_\+\+RESOURCES} & if there is a failure in allocating necessary resources\\
\hline

\hline

Prepares an allocation for interprocess sharing and creates a handle of type hsa\+\_\+amd\+\_\+ipc\+\_\+memory\+\_\+t uniquely identifying the allocation. A handle is valid while the allocation it references remains accessible in any process. In general applications should confirm that a shared memory region has been attached (via hsa\+\_\+amd\+\_\+ipc\+\_\+memory\+\_\+attach) in the remote process prior to releasing that memory in the local process. Repeated calls for the same allocation may, but are not required to, return unique handles. 


\begin{DoxyParams}[1]{Parameters}
\mbox{\texttt{ in}}  & {\em ptr} & Pointer to memory allocated via ROCr APIs to prepare for sharing.\\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{ in}}  & {\em len} & Length in bytes of the allocation to share.\\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{ out}}  & {\em handle} & Process independent identifier referencing the shared allocation.\\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}

\begin{DoxyRetVals}{Return values}
{\em HSA\+\_\+\+STATUS\+\_\+\+SUCCESS} & allocation is prepared for interprocess sharing.\\
\hline
{\em HSA\+\_\+\+STATUS\+\_\+\+ERROR\+\_\+\+NOT\+\_\+\+INITIALIZED} & if HSA is not initialized\\
\hline
{\em HSA\+\_\+\+STATUS\+\_\+\+ERROR\+\_\+\+OUT\+\_\+\+OF\+\_\+\+RESOURCES} & if there is a failure in allocating necessary resources\\
\hline
{\em HSA\+\_\+\+STATUS\+\_\+\+ERROR\+\_\+\+INVALID\+\_\+\+ARGUMENT} & {\ttfamily ptr} does not point to the first byte of an allocation made through ROCr, or len is not the full length of the allocation or handle is NULL. \\
\hline
\end{DoxyRetVals}

Obtains an interprocess sharing handle for a signal. The handle is valid while the signal it references remains valid in any process. In general applications should confirm that the signal has been attached (via hsa\+\_\+amd\+\_\+ipc\+\_\+signal\+\_\+attach) in the remote process prior to destroying that signal in the local process. Repeated calls for the same signal may, but are not required to, return unique handles.

{\footnotesize\ttfamily \mbox{\hyperlink{group__status_gad755322e7ff95456520e8abdbe90d225}{hsa\+\_\+status\+\_\+t}} \mbox{\hyperlink{inc_2hsa__ext__image_8h_a64216765b98b62ee87deb50fd2899836}{HSA\+\_\+\+API}} hsa\+\_\+amd\+\_\+memory\+\_\+async\+\_\+copy (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{void $\ast$}]{dst,  }\item[{\mbox{\hyperlink{group__agentinfo_ga9ce217ef08e7d5e89c8e83ce7b6ef22b}{hsa\+\_\+agent\+\_\+t}}}]{dst\+\_\+agent,  }\item[{const void $\ast$}]{src,  }\item[{\mbox{\hyperlink{group__agentinfo_ga9ce217ef08e7d5e89c8e83ce7b6ef22b}{hsa\+\_\+agent\+\_\+t}}}]{src\+\_\+agent,  }\item[{size\+\_\+t}]{size,  }\item[{uint32\+\_\+t}]{num\+\_\+dep\+\_\+signals,  }\item[{const \mbox{\hyperlink{group__signals_ga0003af605a660e7ef4f89aa555d3b3fc}{hsa\+\_\+signal\+\_\+t}} $\ast$}]{dep\+\_\+signals,  }\item[{\mbox{\hyperlink{group__signals_ga0003af605a660e7ef4f89aa555d3b3fc}{hsa\+\_\+signal\+\_\+t}}}]{completion\+\_\+signal }\end{DoxyParamCaption})}



Asynchronously copy a block of memory from the location pointed to by {\ttfamily src} on the {\ttfamily src\+\_\+agent} to the memory block pointed to by {\ttfamily dst} on the {\ttfamily dst\+\_\+agent}. Because the DMA engines used may not be in the same coherency domain, the caller must ensure that buffers are system-\/level coherent. In general this requires the sending device to have released the buffer to system scope prior to executing the copy API and the receiving device must execute a system scope acquire fence prior to use of the destination buffer. 


\begin{DoxyParams}[1]{Parameters}
\mbox{\texttt{ out}}  & {\em dst} & Buffer where the content is to be copied.\\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{ in}}  & {\em dst\+\_\+agent} & Agent associated with the {\ttfamily dst}. The agent must be able to directly access both the source and destination buffers in their current locations. May be zero in which case the runtime will attempt to discover the destination agent. Discovery may have variable and/or high latency.\\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{ in}}  & {\em src} & A valid pointer to the source of data to be copied. The source buffer must not overlap with the destination buffer, otherwise the copy will succeed but contents of {\ttfamily dst} is undefined.\\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{ in}}  & {\em src\+\_\+agent} & Agent associated with the {\ttfamily src}. The agent must be able to directly access both the source and destination buffers in their current locations. May be zero in which case the runtime will attempt to discover the destination agent. Discovery may have variable and/or high latency.\\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{ in}}  & {\em size} & Number of bytes to copy. If {\ttfamily size} is 0, no copy is performed and the function returns success. Copying a number of bytes larger than the size of the buffers pointed by {\ttfamily dst} or {\ttfamily src} results in undefined behavior.\\
\hline

Asynchronously copy a block of memory from the location pointed to by {\ttfamily src} on the {\ttfamily src\+\_\+agent} to the memory block pointed to by {\ttfamily dst} on the {\ttfamily dst\+\_\+agent} on engine\+\_\+id. 

WARNING\+: Concurrent use of this call with hsa\+\_\+amd\+\_\+memory\+\_\+async\+\_\+copy can result in resource conflicts as HSA runtime will auto assign engines with the latter call. Approach using both calls concurrently with caution.

All param definitions are identical to hsa\+\_\+amd\+\_\+memory\+\_\+async\+\_\+copy with the exception of engine\+\_\+id and force\+\_\+copy\+\_\+on\+\_\+sdma.

\hline
\mbox{\texttt{ in}}  & {\em -\/} & force\+\_\+copy\+\_\+on\+\_\+sdma By default, blit kernel copies are used when dst\+\_\+agent == src\+\_\+agent. Setting this to true will force the copy over SDMA1.\\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}

{\footnotesize\ttfamily \mbox{\hyperlink{group__status_gad755322e7ff95456520e8abdbe90d225}{hsa\+\_\+status\+\_\+t}} \mbox{\hyperlink{inc_2hsa__ext__image_8h_a64216765b98b62ee87deb50fd2899836}{HSA\+\_\+\+API}} hsa\+\_\+amd\+\_\+memory\+\_\+lock (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{void $\ast$}]{host\+\_\+ptr,  }\item[{size\+\_\+t}]{size,  }\item[{\mbox{\hyperlink{group__agentinfo_ga9ce217ef08e7d5e89c8e83ce7b6ef22b}{hsa\+\_\+agent\+\_\+t}} $\ast$}]{agents,  }\item[{int}]{num\+\_\+agent,  }\item[{void $\ast$$\ast$}]{agent\+\_\+ptr }\end{DoxyParamCaption})}



Pin a host pointer allocated by C/\+C++ or OS allocator (i.\+e. ordinary system DRAM) and return a new pointer accessible by the {\ttfamily agents}. If the {\ttfamily host\+\_\+ptr} overlaps with previously locked memory, then the overlap area is kept locked (i.\+e multiple mappings are permitted). In this case, the same input {\ttfamily host\+\_\+ptr} may give different locked {\ttfamily agent\+\_\+ptr} and when it does, they are not necessarily coherent (i.\+e. accessing either {\ttfamily agent\+\_\+ptr} is not equivalent). Accesses to {\ttfamily agent\+\_\+ptr} are coarse grained. 


\begin{DoxyParams}[1]{Parameters}
\mbox{\texttt{ in}}  & {\em host\+\_\+ptr} & A buffer allocated by C/\+C++ or OS allocator.\\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{ in}}  & {\em size} & The size to be locked.\\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{ in}}  & {\em agents} & Array of agent handle to gain access to the {\ttfamily host\+\_\+ptr}. If this parameter is NULL and the {\ttfamily num\+\_\+agent} is 0, all agents in the platform will gain access to the {\ttfamily host\+\_\+ptr}.\\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{ out}}  & {\em agent\+\_\+ptr} & Pointer to the location where to store the new address.\\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}

{\footnotesize\ttfamily \mbox{\hyperlink{group__status_gad755322e7ff95456520e8abdbe90d225}{hsa\+\_\+status\+\_\+t}} \mbox{\hyperlink{inc_2hsa__ext__image_8h_a64216765b98b62ee87deb50fd2899836}{HSA\+\_\+\+API}} hsa\+\_\+amd\+\_\+memory\+\_\+lock\+\_\+to\+\_\+pool (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{void $\ast$}]{host\+\_\+ptr,  }\item[{size\+\_\+t}]{size,  }\item[{\mbox{\hyperlink{group__agentinfo_ga9ce217ef08e7d5e89c8e83ce7b6ef22b}{hsa\+\_\+agent\+\_\+t}} $\ast$}]{agents,  }\item[{int}]{num\+\_\+agent,  }\item[{\mbox{\hyperlink{inc_2hsa__ext__amd_8h_ad7898ef210d13e71a2a490e97083c02d}{hsa\+\_\+amd\+\_\+memory\+\_\+pool\+\_\+t}}}]{pool,  }\item[{uint32\+\_\+t}]{flags,  }\item[{void $\ast$$\ast$}]{agent\+\_\+ptr }\end{DoxyParamCaption})}



Pin a host pointer allocated by C/\+C++ or OS allocator (i.\+e. ordinary system DRAM) and return a new pointer accessible by the {\ttfamily agents}. If the {\ttfamily host\+\_\+ptr} overlaps with previously locked memory, then the overlap area is kept locked (i.\+e. multiple mappings are permitted). In this case, the same input {\ttfamily host\+\_\+ptr} may give different locked {\ttfamily agent\+\_\+ptr} and when it does, they are not necessarily coherent (i.\+e. accessing either {\ttfamily agent\+\_\+ptr} is not equivalent). Acesses to the memory via {\ttfamily agent\+\_\+ptr} have the same access properties as memory allocated from {\ttfamily pool} as determined by \mbox{\hyperlink{inc_2hsa__ext__amd_8h_aa6737782ae0048186b70796e18d5f58f}{hsa\+\_\+amd\+\_\+memory\+\_\+pool\+\_\+get\+\_\+info}} and \mbox{\hyperlink{inc_2hsa__ext__amd_8h_a1a839a4273411c5a1812f72d170fbdc8}{hsa\+\_\+amd\+\_\+agent\+\_\+memory\+\_\+pool\+\_\+get\+\_\+info}} (ex. coarse/fine grain, platform atomic support, link info). Physical composition and placement of the memory (ex. page size, NUMA binding) is not changed. 


\begin{DoxyParams}[1]{Parameters}
\mbox{\texttt{ in}}  & {\em host\+\_\+ptr} & A buffer allocated by C/\+C++ or OS allocator.\\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{ in}}  & {\em size} & The size to be locked.\\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{ in}}  & {\em agents} & Array of agent handle to gain access to the {\ttfamily host\+\_\+ptr}. If this parameter is NULL and the {\ttfamily num\+\_\+agent} is 0, all agents in the platform will gain access to the {\ttfamily host\+\_\+ptr}.\\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{ in}}  & {\em pool} & Global memory pool owned by a CPU agent.\\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{ in}}  & {\em flags} & A bit-\/field that is used to specify allocation directives. Reserved parameter, must be 0.\\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{ out}}  & {\em agent\+\_\+ptr} & Pointer to the location where to store the new address.\\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}

{\em \mbox{\hyperlink{group__status_ggad755322e7ff95456520e8abdbe90d225ae382ea0c9c05cce5a60d0317375159cc}{HSA\+\_\+\+STATUS\+\_\+\+SUCCESS}}} & The function has been executed successfully.\\

\hline

\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{hsa\_amd\_memory\_migrate()}{hsa\_amd\_memory\_migrate()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \mbox{\hyperlink{group__status_gad755322e7ff95456520e8abdbe90d225}{hsa\+\_\+status\+\_\+t}} \mbox{\hyperlink{inc_2hsa__ext__image_8h_a64216765b98b62ee87deb50fd2899836}{HSA\+\_\+\+API}} hsa\+\_\+amd\+\_\+memory\+\_\+migrate (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{const void $\ast$}]{ptr,  }\item[{\mbox{\hyperlink{inc_2hsa__ext__amd_8h_ad7898ef210d13e71a2a490e97083c02d}{hsa\+\_\+amd\+\_\+memory\+\_\+pool\+\_\+t}}}]{memory\+\_\+pool,  }\item[{uint32\+\_\+t}]{flags }\end{DoxyParamCaption})}



Relocate a buffer to a new memory pool. 

When a buffer is migrated, its virtual address remains the same but its physical contents are moved to the indicated memory pool.

After migration, only the agent associated with the destination pool will have access.

The caller is also responsible for ensuring that the allocation in the source memory pool where the buffer is currently located can be migrated to the specified destination memory pool (using \mbox{\hyperlink{inc_2hsa__ext__amd_8h_a98682761a8c6efe2469bc84698ee87d9}{hsa\+\_\+amd\+\_\+memory\+\_\+pool\+\_\+can\+\_\+migrate}} returns a value of true for the source and destination memory pools), otherwise behavior is undefined.

The caller must ensure that the buffer is not accessed while it is migrated.


\begin{DoxyParams}[1]{Parameters}
\mbox{\texttt{ in}}  & {\em ptr} & Buffer to be relocated. The buffer must have been released to system prior to call this API. The buffer will be released to system upon completion.\\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{ in}}  & {\em memory\+\_\+pool} & Memory pool where to place the buffer.\\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{ in}}  & {\em flags} & A bit-\/field that is used to specify migration information. Must be zero.\\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}

\begin{DoxyRetVals}{Return values}
{\em \mbox{\hyperlink{group__status_ggad755322e7ff95456520e8abdbe90d225ae382ea0c9c05cce5a60d0317375159cc}{HSA\+\_\+\+STATUS\+\_\+\+SUCCESS}}} & The function has been executed successfully.\\
\hline

\hline
{\em \mbox{\hyperlink{inc_2hsa__ext__amd_8h_a5e7fba63eebd783231b18f414e8a138fa0b4b4be8220e5a15c667a2171c4babbb}{HSA\+\_\+\+STATUS\+\_\+\+ERROR\+\_\+\+INVALID\+\_\+\+MEMORY\+\_\+\+POOL}}} & The destination memory pool is invalid.\\
\hline

\hline

The memory pool must have the \mbox{\hyperlink{inc_2hsa__ext__amd_8h_a8e7013d1137d7a51b22d4f636fb59506aa5ea40b720c8fdc49459c0fa208ab232}{HSA\+\_\+\+AMD\+\_\+\+MEMORY\+\_\+\+POOL\+\_\+\+INFO\+\_\+\+RUNTIME\+\_\+\+ALLOC\+\_\+\+ALLOWED}} flag set.\\

Query if buffers currently located in some memory pool can be relocated to a destination memory pool. 

If the returned value is non-\/zero, a migration of a buffer to {\ttfamily dst\+\_\+memory\+\_\+pool} using \mbox{\hyperlink{inc_2hsa__ext__amd_8h_a00d18ca270b02ef0d97953eda2276566}{hsa\+\_\+amd\+\_\+memory\+\_\+migrate}} may nevertheless fail due to resource limitations.

\hline

{\footnotesize\ttfamily \mbox{\hyperlink{group__status_gad755322e7ff95456520e8abdbe90d225}{hsa\+\_\+status\+\_\+t}} hsa\+\_\+amd\+\_\+portable\+\_\+close\+\_\+dmabuf (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{int}]{dmabuf }\end{DoxyParamCaption})}



Closes an OS specific, vendor neutral, handle to a memory allocation. 

Closes an OS specific handle to GPU agent memory.

Applications should close a handle after imports are complete. The handle is not required to remain open for the lifetime of imported mappings. The referenced allocation will remain valid until all handles and mappings are closed.


\begin{DoxyParams}[1]{Parameters}
\mbox{\texttt{ in}}  & {\em dmabuf} & Handle to be closed.\\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}

\begin{DoxyRetVals}{Return values}

{\em \mbox{\hyperlink{group__status_ggad755322e7ff95456520e8abdbe90d225a6406af88203fcbec4179fbb71cc66b65}{HSA\+\_\+\+STATUS\+\_\+\+ERROR\+\_\+\+RESOURCE\+\_\+\+FREE}}} & A generic error was encountered when closing the handle. \doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{hsa\_amd\_portable\_export\_dmabuf()}{hsa\_amd\_portable\_export\_dmabuf()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \mbox{\hyperlink{group__status_gad755322e7ff95456520e8abdbe90d225}{hsa\+\_\+status\+\_\+t}} hsa\+\_\+amd\+\_\+portable\+\_\+export\+\_\+dmabuf (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{const void $\ast$}]{ptr,  }\item[{size\+\_\+t}]{size,  }\item[{int $\ast$}]{dmabuf,  }\item[{uint64\+\_\+t $\ast$}]{offset }\end{DoxyParamCaption})}



Obtains an OS specific, vendor neutral, handle to a memory allocation. 

Obtains an OS specific handle to GPU agent memory. The memory must be part of a single allocation from an hsa\+\_\+amd\+\_\+memory\+\_\+pool\+\_\+t exposed by a GPU Agent. The handle may be used with other APIs (e.\+g. Vulkan) to obtain shared access to the allocation.

Shared access to the memory is not guaranteed to be fine grain coherent even if the allocation exported is from a fine grain pool. The shared memory consistency model will be no stronger than the model exported from, consult the importing API to determine the final consistency model.

The allocation\textquotesingle{}s memory remains valid as long as the handle and any mapping of the handle remains valid. When the handle and all mappings are closed the backing memory will be released for reuse.


\begin{DoxyParams}[1]{Parameters}
\mbox{\texttt{ in}}  & {\em ptr} & Pointer to the allocation being exported.\\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{ in}}  & {\em size} & Size in bytes to export following {\ttfamily ptr}. The entire range being exported must be contained within a single allocation.\\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{ out}}  & {\em dmabuf} & Pointer to a dma-\/buf file descriptor holding a reference to the allocation. Contents will not be altered in the event of failure.\\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{ out}}  & {\em offset} & Offset in bytes into the memory referenced by the dma-\/buf object at which {\ttfamily ptr} resides. Contents will not be altered in the event of failure.\\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}

\begin{DoxyRetVals}{Return values}
{\em \mbox{\hyperlink{group__status_ggad755322e7ff95456520e8abdbe90d225ae382ea0c9c05cce5a60d0317375159cc}{HSA\+\_\+\+STATUS\+\_\+\+SUCCESS}}} & Export completed successfully.\\
\hline
{\em \mbox{\hyperlink{group__status_ggad755322e7ff95456520e8abdbe90d225a34ea59ade5bfce95eee935238a99f5b5}{HSA\+\_\+\+STATUS\+\_\+\+ERROR\+\_\+\+NOT\+\_\+\+INITIALIZED}}} & The HSA runtime has not been initialized.\\
\hline
{\em \mbox{\hyperlink{group__status_ggad755322e7ff95456520e8abdbe90d225ac7d3651f75107d2a6a8ba3b25683c030}{HSA\+\_\+\+STATUS\+\_\+\+ERROR\+\_\+\+INVALID\+\_\+\+ARGUMENT}}} & One or more arguments is NULL.\\
\hline
{\em \mbox{\hyperlink{group__status_ggad755322e7ff95456520e8abdbe90d225ac818189ff640d38ce13558e72daddb75}{HSA\+\_\+\+STATUS\+\_\+\+ERROR\+\_\+\+INVALID\+\_\+\+ALLOCATION}}} & The address range described by {\ttfamily ptr} and {\ttfamily size} are not contained within a single allocation.\\
\hline
{\em \mbox{\hyperlink{group__status_ggad755322e7ff95456520e8abdbe90d225a3a5d835c109c2d0ad5b9c2771e133e5d}{HSA\+\_\+\+STATUS\+\_\+\+ERROR\+\_\+\+INVALID\+\_\+\+AGENT}}} & The allocation described by {\ttfamily ptr} and {\ttfamily size} was allocated on a device which can not export memory.\\
\hline
{\em \mbox{\hyperlink{group__status_ggad755322e7ff95456520e8abdbe90d225a1a77fcf36d0d140874c4361ab093eff7}{HSA\+\_\+\+STATUS\+\_\+\+ERROR\+\_\+\+OUT\+\_\+\+OF\+\_\+\+RESOURCES}}} & The return file descriptor, {\ttfamily dmabuf}, could not be created. \\
\hline
\end{DoxyRetVals}

Enable or disable asynchronous memory copy profiling. 

The runtime will provide the copy processing start timestamp and completion timestamp of each call to hsa\+\_\+amd\+\_\+memory\+\_\+async\+\_\+copy if the async copy profiling is enabled prior to the call to hsa\+\_\+amd\+\_\+memory\+\_\+async\+\_\+copy. The completion signal object is used to hold the last async copy start and end timestamp. The client can retrieve these timestamps via call to hsa\+\_\+amd\+\_\+profiling\+\_\+get\+\_\+async\+\_\+copy\+\_\+time.


\begin{DoxyParams}[1]{Parameters}
\mbox{\texttt{ in}}  & {\em enable} & True to enable profiling. False to disable profiling.\\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}

\begin{DoxyRetVals}{Return values}
{\em \mbox{\hyperlink{group__status_ggad755322e7ff95456520e8abdbe90d225ae382ea0c9c05cce5a60d0317375159cc}{HSA\+\_\+\+STATUS\+\_\+\+SUCCESS}}} & The function has been executed successfully.\\

\hline
\end{DoxyRetVals}

Set a queue\textquotesingle{}s CU affinity mask. 

Enables the queue to run on only selected CUs. The given mask is combined by bitwise AND with any device wide mask in HSA\+\_\+\+CU\+\_\+\+MASK before being applied. If num\+\_\+cu\+\_\+mask\+\_\+count is 0 then the request is interpreted as a request to enable all CUs and no cu\+\_\+mask array need be given.

\hline
\mbox{\texttt{ in}}  & {\em cu\+\_\+mask} & Bit-\/vector representing the CU mask.\\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}

Registers a deallocation notifier monitoring for release of agent accessible address {\ttfamily ptr}. If successful, {\ttfamily callback} will be invoked when {\ttfamily ptr} is removed from accessibility from all agents. 

Notification callbacks are automatically deregistered when they are invoked.

Note\+: The current version supports notifications of address release originating from \mbox{\hyperlink{inc_2hsa__ext__amd_8h_ad7d56018302f71b17e46fe30c92aa4be}{hsa\+\_\+amd\+\_\+memory\+\_\+pool\+\_\+free}}. Support for other address release APIs will follow.

\mbox{\texttt{ in}}  & {\em ptr} & Agent accessible address to monitor for deallocation. Passed to {\ttfamily callback}.\\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{ in}}  & {\em callback} & Notifier to be invoked when {\ttfamily ptr} is released from agent accessibility.\\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{ in}}  & {\em user\+\_\+data} & User provided value passed to {\ttfamily callback}. May be NULL.\\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}

\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{hsa\_amd\_signal\_async\_handler()}{hsa\_amd\_signal\_async\_handler()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \mbox{\hyperlink{group__status_gad755322e7ff95456520e8abdbe90d225}{hsa\+\_\+status\+\_\+t}} \mbox{\hyperlink{inc_2hsa__ext__image_8h_a64216765b98b62ee87deb50fd2899836}{HSA\+\_\+\+API}} hsa\+\_\+amd\+\_\+signal\+\_\+async\+\_\+handler (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{\mbox{\hyperlink{group__signals_ga0003af605a660e7ef4f89aa555d3b3fc}{hsa\+\_\+signal\+\_\+t}}}]{signal,  }\item[{\mbox{\hyperlink{group__signals_gab7190fcff48c6dbeded341389ed17c8d}{hsa\+\_\+signal\+\_\+condition\+\_\+t}}}]{cond,  }\item[{\mbox{\hyperlink{group__signals_ga67ca2818879c9990e1b5f1b14ce7ed27}{hsa\+\_\+signal\+\_\+value\+\_\+t}}}]{value,  }\item[{\mbox{\hyperlink{inc_2hsa__ext__amd_8h_ad52f140818ba83c11558d0c50e20bf2c}{hsa\+\_\+amd\+\_\+signal\+\_\+handler}}}]{handler,  }\item[{void $\ast$}]{arg }\end{DoxyParamCaption})}



Register asynchronous signal handler function. 

Allows registering a callback function and user provided value with a signal and wait condition. The callback will be invoked if the associated signal and wait condition are satisfied. Callbacks will be invoked serially but in an arbitrary order so callbacks should be independent of each other. After being invoked a callback may continue to wait for its associated signal and condition and, possibly, be invoked again. Or the callback may stop waiting. If the callback returns true then it will continue waiting and may be called again. If false then the callback will not wait again and will not be called again for the associated signal and condition. It is possible to register the same callback multiple times with the same or different signals and/or conditions. Each registration of the callback will be treated entirely independently.


\begin{DoxyParams}[1]{Parameters}
\mbox{\texttt{ in}}  & {\em signal} & hsa signal to be asynchronously monitored\\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{ in}}  & {\em cond} & condition value to monitor for\\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{ in}}  & {\em value} & signal value used in condition expression\\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{ in}}  & {\em handler} & asynchronous signal handler invoked when signal\textquotesingle{}s condition is met\\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{ in}}  & {\em arg} & user provided value which is provided to handler when handler is invoked\\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}

\begin{DoxyRetVals}{Return values}
{\em \mbox{\hyperlink{group__status_ggad755322e7ff95456520e8abdbe90d225ae382ea0c9c05cce5a60d0317375159cc}{HSA\+\_\+\+STATUS\+\_\+\+SUCCESS}}} & The function has been executed successfully.\\
\hline
{\em \mbox{\hyperlink{group__status_ggad755322e7ff95456520e8abdbe90d225a34ea59ade5bfce95eee935238a99f5b5}{HSA\+\_\+\+STATUS\+\_\+\+ERROR\+\_\+\+NOT\+\_\+\+INITIALIZED}}} & The HSA runtime has not been initialized.\\
\hline
{\em \mbox{\hyperlink{group__status_ggad755322e7ff95456520e8abdbe90d225a7b4c8c0d4c99a1fe966abc2d39b575fe}{HSA\+\_\+\+STATUS\+\_\+\+ERROR\+\_\+\+INVALID\+\_\+\+SIGNAL}}} & signal is not a valid hsa\+\_\+signal\+\_\+t\\
\hline
{\em \mbox{\hyperlink{group__status_ggad755322e7ff95456520e8abdbe90d225ac7d3651f75107d2a6a8ba3b25683c030}{HSA\+\_\+\+STATUS\+\_\+\+ERROR\+\_\+\+INVALID\+\_\+\+ARGUMENT}}} & handler is invalid (NULL)\\
\hline
{\em \mbox{\hyperlink{group__status_ggad755322e7ff95456520e8abdbe90d225a1a77fcf36d0d140874c4361ab093eff7}{HSA\+\_\+\+STATUS\+\_\+\+ERROR\+\_\+\+OUT\+\_\+\+OF\+\_\+\+RESOURCES}}} & The HSA runtime is out of resources or blocking signals are not supported by the HSA driver component. \\
\hline
\end{DoxyRetVals}

Create a signal with specific attributes. 


\begin{DoxyParams}[1]{Parameters}

{\footnotesize\ttfamily \mbox{\hyperlink{group__status_gad755322e7ff95456520e8abdbe90d225}{hsa\+\_\+status\+\_\+t}} hsa\+\_\+amd\+\_\+signal\+\_\+value\+\_\+pointer (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{\mbox{\hyperlink{group__signals_ga0003af605a660e7ef4f89aa555d3b3fc}{hsa\+\_\+signal\+\_\+t}}}]{signal,  }\item[{volatile \mbox{\hyperlink{group__signals_ga67ca2818879c9990e1b5f1b14ce7ed27}{hsa\+\_\+signal\+\_\+value\+\_\+t}} $\ast$$\ast$}]{value\+\_\+ptr }\end{DoxyParamCaption})}



Returns a pointer to the value of a signal. 

Use of this API does not modify the lifetime of \+::signal and any hsa\+\_\+signal\+\_\+value\+\_\+t retrieved by this API has lifetime equal to that of \+::signal.

This API is intended for partial interoperability with non-\/\+HSA compatible devices and should not be used where HSA interfaces are available.

Use of the signal value must comply with use restritions of \+::signal. Use may result in data races if the operations performed are not platform atomic. Use with HSA\+\_\+\+AMD\+\_\+\+SIGNAL\+\_\+\+AMD\+\_\+\+GPU\+\_\+\+ONLY or HSA\+\_\+\+AMD\+\_\+\+SIGNAL\+\_\+\+IPC attributed signals is required.

\mbox{\texttt{ in}}  & {\em Signal} & handle to extract the signal value pointer from.\\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{ out}}  & {\em Location} & where the extracted signal value pointer will be placed.\\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}

Wait for any signal-\/condition pair to be satisfied. 

Allows waiting for any of several signal and conditions pairs to be satisfied. The function returns the index into the list of signals of the first satisfying signal-\/condition pair. The value of the satisfying signal\textquotesingle{}s value is returned in satisfying\+\_\+value unless satisfying\+\_\+value is NULL. This function provides only relaxed memory semantics. \mbox{\Hypertarget{inc_2hsa__ext__amd_8h_a2ae38616942306c43e77687385b31936}\label{inc_2hsa__ext__amd_8h_a2ae38616942306c43e77687385b31936}}

\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{hsa\_amd\_spm\_set\_dest\_buffer()}{hsa\_amd\_spm\_set\_dest\_buffer()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \mbox{\hyperlink{group__status_gad755322e7ff95456520e8abdbe90d225}{hsa\+\_\+status\+\_\+t}} hsa\+\_\+amd\+\_\+spm\+\_\+set\+\_\+dest\+\_\+buffer (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{\mbox{\hyperlink{group__agentinfo_ga9ce217ef08e7d5e89c8e83ce7b6ef22b}{hsa\+\_\+agent\+\_\+t}}}]{preferred\+\_\+agent,  }\item[{size\+\_\+t}]{size\+\_\+in\+\_\+bytes,  }\item[{uint32\+\_\+t $\ast$}]{timeout,  }\item[{uint32\+\_\+t $\ast$}]{size\+\_\+copied,  }\item[{void $\ast$}]{dest,  }\item[{bool $\ast$}]{is\+\_\+data\+\_\+loss }\end{DoxyParamCaption})}



Set up the current destination user mode buffer for stream performance counter data. KFD will start writing SPM data into the destination buffer. KFD will continue to copy data into the current destination buffer until any of the following functions are called. 


\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item hsa\+\_\+amd\+\_\+spm\+\_\+release
\item hsa\+\_\+amd\+\_\+spm\+\_\+set\+\_\+dest\+\_\+buffer with dest set to NULL
\item hsa\+\_\+amd\+\_\+spm\+\_\+set\+\_\+dest\+\_\+buffer with dest set to a new buffer
\end{DoxyItemize}

if {\ttfamily timeout} is non-\/0, the call will wait for up to {\ttfamily timeout} ms for the previous buffer to be filled. If previous buffer to be filled before timeout, the {\ttfamily timeout} will be updated value with the time remaining. If the timeout is exceeded, the function copies any partial data available into the previous user buffer and returns success. User should not access destination data while KFD is copying data. If the previous destination buffer was full, then {\ttfamily is\+\_\+data\+\_\+loss} flag is set. {\ttfamily dest} is CPU accessible memory. It could be malloc\textquotesingle{}ed memory or host allocated memory


\begin{DoxyParams}[1]{Parameters}
\mbox{\texttt{ in}}  & {\em preferred\+\_\+agent} & Agent on which to set the dest buffer\\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{ in}}  & {\em size\+\_\+in\+\_\+bytes} & size of the buffer\\
\hline
 & {\em \mbox{[}in/out\mbox{]}} & timeout timeout in milliseconds\\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{ out}}  & {\em size\+\_\+copied} & number of bytes copied\\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{ in}}  & {\em dest} & destination address. Set to NULL to stop copy on previous buffer\\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{ out}}  & {\em is\+\_\+data\+\_\+loss} & true is data was lost \\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}

{\footnotesize\ttfamily \mbox{\hyperlink{group__status_gad755322e7ff95456520e8abdbe90d225}{hsa\+\_\+status\+\_\+t}} hsa\+\_\+amd\+\_\+svm\+\_\+prefetch\+\_\+async (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{void $\ast$}]{ptr,  }\item[{size\+\_\+t}]{size,  }\item[{\mbox{\hyperlink{group__agentinfo_ga9ce217ef08e7d5e89c8e83ce7b6ef22b}{hsa\+\_\+agent\+\_\+t}}}]{agent,  }\item[{uint32\+\_\+t}]{num\+\_\+dep\+\_\+signals,  }\item[{const \mbox{\hyperlink{group__signals_ga0003af605a660e7ef4f89aa555d3b3fc}{hsa\+\_\+signal\+\_\+t}} $\ast$}]{dep\+\_\+signals,  }\item[{\mbox{\hyperlink{group__signals_ga0003af605a660e7ef4f89aa555d3b3fc}{hsa\+\_\+signal\+\_\+t}}}]{completion\+\_\+signal }\end{DoxyParamCaption})}



Asynchronously migrates memory to an agent. 

Schedules memory migration to {\ttfamily agent} when {\ttfamily dep\+\_\+signals} have been observed equal to zero. {\ttfamily completion\+\_\+signal} will decrement when the migration is complete.


\begin{DoxyParams}[1]{Parameters}
\mbox{\texttt{ in}}  & {\em ptr} & Will be aligned down to nearest page boundary.\\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{ in}}  & {\em size} & Will be aligned up to nearest page boundary.\\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{ in}}  & {\em agent} & Agent to migrate to.\\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{ in}}  & {\em num\+\_\+dep\+\_\+signals} & Number of dependent signals. Can be 0.\\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{ in}}  & {\em dep\+\_\+signals} & List of signals that must be waited on before the migration operation starts. The migration will start after every signal has been observed with the value 0. If {\ttfamily num\+\_\+dep\+\_\+signals} is 0, this argument is ignored.\\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{ in}}  & {\em completion\+\_\+signal} & Signal used to indicate completion of the migration operation. When the migration operation is finished, the value of the signal is decremented. The runtime indicates that an error has occurred during the copy operation by setting the value of the completion signal to a negative number. If no completion signal is required this handle may be null. \\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}
